  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))
        \vdash E_1=E_2
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))
        \vdash E_1=E_2
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeRecursionUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{E_1,E_2\colon B\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,6]{E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6]{E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6]{%
      E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{7,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,9}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[11]{S\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{T\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land E_1(T)=E_2(T)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,11,12]{%
      E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))}{%
      \rRE{12,\rUE{\rRE{11,\rUE{4}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5,11,12]{%
      E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{%
      \rRE{12,\rUE{\rRE{11,\rUE{5}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      g(E_1(S),E_1(T))=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{%
      \rIE{\rAEa{13},\rAEb{13}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5,11,12,13]{%
      E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rIE{14,16,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5,11,12]{%
      E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land E_1(T)=E_2(T)\rightarrow
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rRI{13,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,\Bigl(
          E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad E_1(T)=E_2(T)\rightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad\quad=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{11,\rUI{\rRI{12,18}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5]{%
      \forall Q\in B\,E_1(Q)=E_2(Q)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeStructuralInduction}[thm]{10,19}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{E_1=E_2}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{1,20}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{E\colon B\to X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionTyping}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionLeafEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionNodeEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall a\in A\,
          E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall a\in A\,
          E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,
          E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,
          E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))\vdash E_1=E_2
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUniqueness}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists!E\colon B\to X\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
        &\quad \forall a\in A\,
          E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\quad \forall S,T\in B\,
          E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad=g(E(S),E(T))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \UEI{3,4,5,6}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\section{Positionsadressen und der zugehörige Baum}

Ein Baumcode ist ein Wort und kann daher gleiche Teilbaumcodes an mehreren
Stellen enthalten. Solche Vorkommen dürfen nicht als ein Knoten
identifiziert werden. Wir verwenden deshalb \emph{Positionsadressen}: Das
Leerwort bezeichnet die Wurzel, und das Voranstellen von (0) bzw. (1)
bezeichnet den Schritt zum linken bzw. rechten Teilbaum. Die Knoten des
folgenden Graphen sind somit Vorkommen, nicht Teilbaumwerte.

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Binäre Positionsadressen]{%
  \BinaryAddresses\coloneqq\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}%
}{BinaryAddressWordSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{\BinaryAddresses\dsep p\dsep q\dsep i}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Binäre Adressen}{\BinaryAddresses}
}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Voranstellen eines Adressbits]{%
  i\in\{0,1\}\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\AddressPrefixMap{i}\colon\BinaryAddresses\to\BinaryAddresses,\\[-2pt]
    &\forall p\in\BinaryAddresses\,
      \bigl(\AddressPrefix{i}{p}\coloneqq
        \WordConcat{\LetterWord{i}}{p}\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{BinaryAddressPrefixDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{i\dsep p\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\AddressPrefixMap{i}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Adresspräfix}{\AddressPrefixMap{i}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{i\in\{0,1\}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{p\in\BinaryAddresses}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{i}\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{p\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressWordSetDef}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\WordConcat{\LetterWord i}p\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}}{\FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall p\in\BinaryAddresses\,
      \bigl(\WordConcat{\LetterWord{i}}{p}\in\BinaryAddresses\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{2,5}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \AddressPrefixMap{i}\colon\BinaryAddresses\to\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{6}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Trennung und Injektivität der Adresspräfixe]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  &\text{(i)}\;i,j\in\{0,1\}\dsep p,q\in\BinaryAddresses\dsep
    \AddressPrefix{i}{p}=\AddressPrefix{j}{q}
    \vdash i=j\land p=q,\\[-2pt]
  &\text{(ii)}\;
    \AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cap\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]=\varnothing.
  \end{aligned}%
}{BinaryAddressPrefixSeparation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{i\dsep j\dsep p\dsep q\dsep X\dsep Y}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\AddressPrefixMap{0}\dsep\AddressPrefixMap{1}}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Gemeinsame Präfixgleichheit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &i,j\in\{0,1\}\dsep p,q\in\BinaryAddresses\dsep
      \AddressPrefix{i}{p}=\AddressPrefix{j}{q}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad\vdash i=j\land p=q
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    i,j\in\{0,1\}\dsep p,q\in\BinaryAddresses\dsep
    \AddressPrefix{i}{p}=\AddressPrefix{j}{q}
    \vdash i=j\land p=q
  }[BinaryAddressPrefixJointInjective]
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{i,j\in\{0,1\}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{p,q\in\BinaryAddresses}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{\AddressPrefix{i}{p}=\AddressPrefix{j}{q}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{\LetterWord{i}}=1=
      \WordLength{\LetterWord{j}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{\LetterWord{i}=\LetterWord{j}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationFixedCutLeft}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{1},2,4,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{p=q}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationFixedCutRight}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{1},2,4,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{i=j}{\FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordInjectivity}[thm]{1,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{i=j\land p=q}{\rAI{7,6}}
\closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Disjunktheit der beiden Präfixbilder]{%
    \AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cap\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]=\varnothing
  }{\AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cap\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]=\varnothing}%
    [BinaryAddressPrefixImagesDisjoint]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      z\in\AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cap\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists p\in X\,(z=\AddressPrefix{0}{p})\land
      \exists q\in Y\,(z=\AddressPrefix{1}{q})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\cap B\eqvdash x\in A\land x\in B}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      p\in X\land q\in Y\land
      z=\AddressPrefix{0}{p}\land z=\AddressPrefix{1}{q}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \AddressPrefix{0}{p}=\AddressPrefix{1}{q}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep a=c\vdash b=c}[thm]{\rAEn{3},\rAEn{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{0=1}{%
      \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressPrefixJointInjective}[thm]{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{0\neq1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZeroNeOne}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{\bot}{\rBI{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\bot}{\rEE{2,3,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cap\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall x\,x\notin A\vdash A=\varnothing}[thm]{%
        \rUI{\rRI{1,8}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die beiden Adresskinder sind verschieden]{%
  p\in\BinaryAddresses\vdash
    \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\neq\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}%
}{BinaryAddressChildrenDistinct}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{p\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{p\in\BinaryAddresses}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{p\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressWordSetDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{0,1\in\{0,1\}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a,b\}}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{b\in\{a,b\}}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\LetterWord0\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordFinite}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{3}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\LetterWord1\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordFinite}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{3}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\WordLength p=\WordLength p}{\rII}
  \proofstepwidestar[7]{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\LetterWord0=\LetterWord1}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationFixedCutRight}[thm]{2,2,4,5,6,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{0=1}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordInjectivity}[thm]{3,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{0\neq1}{\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZeroNeOne}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\bot}{\rBI{10,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\neq\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}}{%
    \rCI{7,11}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Pfropfen zweier Adressmengen]{%
  X,Y\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\vdash
  \AddressGraft{X}{Y}\coloneqq
  \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\cup
  \AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cup\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]%
}{BinaryAddressGraftDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{X\dsep Y\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\Gamma}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Adresspfropfung}{\Gamma}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die Adresspfropfung ist eine binäre Operation]{%
  \mathsf{BinOp}\bigl(\powerset(\BinaryAddresses),\Gamma\bigr)%
}{BinaryAddressGraftBinaryOperation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{X\dsep Y\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\Gamma}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X,Y\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{0,1\in\{0,1\}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a,b\}}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{b\in\{a,b\}}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \AddressPrefixMap{0},\AddressPrefixMap{1}\colon
      \BinaryAddresses\to\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressPrefixDef}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \AddressPrefixMap{0}[X],\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]
      \subseteq\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\dsep F\colon A\to B
      \vdash F[C]\subseteq B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{1},3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordFinite}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\subseteq\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash\{a\}\subseteq A}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cup\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]
      \subseteq\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq C\dsep B\subseteq C
      \vdash A\cup B\subseteq C}[thm]{\rAEa{4},\rAEb{4}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \AddressGraft{X}{Y}\subseteq\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraftDef}[def]{1,
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq C\dsep B\subseteq C
        \vdash A\cup B\subseteq C}[thm]{6,7}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \AddressGraft{X}{Y}\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}\bigl(\powerset(\BinaryAddresses),\Gamma\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationDef}[def]{9}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Volle endliche Präfixmenge]{%
  \begin{aligned}
  \GoodAddressSet{P}\coloneqq{}&
    P\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\land\Finite P\land
    \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in P\land{}\\[-2pt]
  &\Bigl(\forall q\in P\setminus\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\,
    \exists p\in P\,\exists i\in\{0,1\}\,
      q=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}\Bigr)\land{}\\[-2pt]
  &\forall p\in P\,\Bigl(
    \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in P\leftrightarrow
    \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in P\Bigr).
  \end{aligned}%
}{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{P\dsep p\dsep q\dsep i\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Prädikate}{\GoodAddressSet{P}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Volle Präfixmenge}{\GoodAddressSet{P}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Grund- und Pfropfschritt voller Präfixmengen]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  &\text{(i)}\;\GoodAddressSet{\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}},\\[-2pt]
  &\text{(ii)}\;\GoodAddressSet X\dsep\GoodAddressSet Y
    \vdash\GoodAddressSet{\AddressGraft X Y}.
  \end{aligned}%
}{GoodBinaryAddressSetConstructors}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{X\dsep Y\dsep P\dsep p\dsep q\dsep i}
  \DeltaRow{Prädikate}{\GoodAddressSet{\,\cdot\,}}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Grundmenge]{%
    \GoodAddressSet{\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}
  }{\GoodAddressSet{\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}}%
    [GoodBinaryAddressSetLeaf]
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash\{a\}\subseteq A}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordFinite}[thm]}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{\Finite{\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteSingleton}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a\}}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      q\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\setminus
        \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{q\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\vdash x\in A}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{q\notin\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\vdash x\notin B}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{\bot}{\rBI{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists p\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\,
        \exists i\in\{0,1\}\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad q=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\bot\vdash P}[thm]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall q\in
        \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\setminus
          \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\exists p\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\,
        \exists i\in\{0,1\}\,
          q=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}
      \end{aligned}}{\rUI{\rRI{4,8}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \WordConcat{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{\LetterWord0}
      \notin\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \WordConcat{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{\LetterWord1}
      \notin\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      p\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{p=\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}
        \notin\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{\rIE{13,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}
        \notin\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{\rIE{13,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\neg\bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}
        \in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\bigr)\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\neg\bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}
        \in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rAI{14,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\Bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}
        \in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\land
        \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}
        \in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\Bigr)\lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(\neg\bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}
        \in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\bigr)\land
        \neg\bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}
        \in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rOIb{16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}
      \leftrightarrow
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{P \leftrightarrow Q \eqvdash
        (P \land Q) \lor (\neg P \land \neg Q)}[thm]{17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \forall p\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\,\Bigl(
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}
      \leftrightarrow
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\Bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{12,18}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \GoodAddressSet{\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1,2,3,9,19}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Pfropfung]{%
    \GoodAddressSet X\dsep\GoodAddressSet Y
      \vdash\GoodAddressSet{\AddressGraft X Y}
  }{%
    \GoodAddressSet X\dsep\GoodAddressSet Y
      \vdash\GoodAddressSet{\AddressGraft X Y}
  }[GoodBinaryAddressSetGraft]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\GoodAddressSet Y}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X,Y\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Finite X\land\Finite Y}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \AddressPrefixMap{0},\AddressPrefixMap{1}\colon
      \BinaryAddresses\to\BinaryAddresses}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressPrefixDef}[def]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a,b\}}[thm],%
        \FormulaRefAuto{b\in\{a,b\}}[thm]}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \Finite{\AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]}\land
      \Finite{\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteImage}[thm]{3,5,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{\Finite{\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteSingleton}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \Finite{\bigl(\AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\cup
        \AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]\bigr)}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteUnion}[thm]{\rAEa{6},\rAEb{6}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Finite{\AddressGraft X Y}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteUnion}[thm]{7,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \AddressGraft X Y\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraftBinaryOperation}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in\AddressGraft X Y}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraftDef}[def]{3,
        \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash x\in A\cup B}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash x\in A\cup B}[thm]{%
            \FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a\}}[thm]}}}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall q\in\AddressGraft X Y\setminus
        \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\,\exists p\in\AddressGraft X Y\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\exists i\in\{0,1\}\,
        q=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Fallunterscheidung nach
        \(q\in\AddressPrefixMap{0}[X]\) oder
        \(q\in\AddressPrefixMap{1}[Y]\);\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecomposition}[thm],
        \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationAssociative}[thm]}}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall p\in\AddressGraft X Y\,\Bigl(
        \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in\AddressGraft X Y\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\leftrightarrow
        \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in\AddressGraft X Y\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Für die Wurzel liegen beide Kinder vor;\\
        in den beiden disjunkten Präfixbildern folgt die Äquivalenz aus\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressPrefixImagesDisjoint}[thm],\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationAssociative}[thm]\\
        und den beiden Prämissen}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\GoodAddressSet{\AddressGraft X Y}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{10,9,11,12,13}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Konstante Blattadressabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  &c_A\colon A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses),\\[-2pt]
  &\forall a\in A\,
    \bigl(c_A(a)\coloneqq\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreePositionLeafMapDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{c_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Blattadressabbildung}{c_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in\BinaryAddresses}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordFinite}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\in\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash\{a\}\subseteq A}[thm]{1}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\in
        \powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\bigr)}{\rUI{\rRI{2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{c_A\colon A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Positionsabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  &\exists!P\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
  &\quad\forall a\in A\,
      P(\LeafTree A a)=\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\land{}\\[-2pt]
  &\quad\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\,
      P(\NodeTree A S T)=\AddressGraft{P(S)}{P(T)}\Bigr).
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreePositionMapExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{P\dsep c_A\dsep\Gamma}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{c_A\colon A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionLeafMapDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}\bigl(\powerset(\BinaryAddresses),\Gamma\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraftBinaryOperation}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{\Gamma}\colon
      \powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\times\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)
      \to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestarbreak[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\exists!P\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad\forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree A a)=c_A(a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\,
      P(\NodeTree A S T)=
      \BinaryOpFunction{\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{\Gamma}
        (P(S),P(T))\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{1,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \exists!P\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree A a)=
        \{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\land
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\,
        P(\NodeTree A S T)=\AddressGraft{P(S)}{P(T)}\Bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionLeafMapDef}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraftDef}[def]{4}}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Positionsmenge eines Baumcodes]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  &\operatorname{Pos}_A\coloneqq
    \iota P\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
  &\quad\forall a\in A\,
      P(\LeafTree A a)=\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}\land{}\\[-2pt]
  &\quad\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\,
      P(\NodeTree A S T)=\AddressGraft{P(S)}{P(T)}\Bigr).
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreePositionSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep R\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{Pos}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Positionsmenge}{\TreePositions A R}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Rekursionsgleichungen der Positionsmenge]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  &\text{(i)}\;\operatorname{Pos}_A\colon
    \BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses),\\[-2pt]
  &\text{(ii)}\;a\in A\vdash
    \TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}=\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\},\\[-2pt]
  &\text{(iii)}\;S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\vdash
    \TreePositions A{\NodeTree A S T}=
      \AddressGraft{\TreePositions A S}{\TreePositions A T}.
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreePositionSetEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T\dsep\BinaryAddresses}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{Pos}_A}
}
Für diesen Beweis bezeichnet \(\Phi(P)\) die Konjunktion der
Typisierung \(P\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)\)
mit den beiden allquantifizierten Rekursionsgleichungen aus
\FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionMapExistenceUnique}[thm].
\newcommand{\BXXVIIProofTreePositionIota}{%
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\exists!P\,\Phi(P)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionMapExistenceUnique}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\operatorname{Pos}_A=\iota P\,\Phi(P)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionSetDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Phi(\operatorname{Pos}_A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{1,2}}}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung]{%
    \operatorname{Pos}_A\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)
  }{\operatorname{Pos}_A\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}%
    [TreePositionSetTyping]
    \BXXVIIProofTreePositionIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \operatorname{Pos}_A\colon\BracketTreeSet A\to\powerset(\BinaryAddresses)}{%
      \rAEa{3}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
  \proofpartwideindR[Blattgleichung]{%
    a\in A\vdash
      \TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}=\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}
  }{a\in A\vdash
      \TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}=\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}%
    [TreePositionSetLeafEquation]
    \BXXVIIProofTreePositionIota
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}=\{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\}}{%
      \rRE{4,\rUE{\rAEa{\rAEb{3}}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
  \proofpartwideindR[Knotengleichung]{%
    S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\vdash
      \TreePositions A{\NodeTree A S T}=
        \AddressGraft{\TreePositions A S}{\TreePositions A T}
  }{S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\vdash
      \TreePositions A{\NodeTree A S T}=
        \AddressGraft{\TreePositions A S}{\TreePositions A T}}%
    [TreePositionSetNodeEquation]
    \BXXVIIProofTreePositionIota
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \TreePositions A{\NodeTree A S T}=
        \AddressGraft{\TreePositions A S}{\TreePositions A T}}{%
      \rRE{4,\rUE{\rUE{\rAEb{\rAEb{3}}}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Positionsmengen sind volle endliche Präfixmengen]{%
  R\in\BracketTreeSet A\vdash\GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A R}%
}{TreePositionSetIsGood}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep R\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}

}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Blatt]{%
    \forall a\in A\,\GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}}

      \GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}}}{\rIE{2,3}}

      \forall a\in A\,\GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A{\LeafTree A a}}}{%

  \proofpartwideindR[Knoten]{%

    \end{aligned}
  }{%

    &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\,\Bigl(

  }[TreePositionSetGoodNode]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A}{\rA}

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \TreePositions A{\NodeTree A S T}=

    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \GoodAddressSet{\AddressGraft{\TreePositions A S}{\TreePositions A T}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetGraft}[thm]{\rAEa{2},\rAEb{2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A{\NodeTree A S T}}}{\rIE{3,4}}

    \proofstepwidestar[]{\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet A\,(\cdots)}{%

  \proofpartwide{Strukturinduktion}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{R\in\BracketTreeSet A}{\rA}

        \GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A R}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeStructuralInduction}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionSetGoodLeaf}[thm],
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionSetGoodNode}[thm]}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A R}}{\rRE{1,2}}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Adressgraph und geordnete Kindoperatoren]{%
  \GoodAddressSet P\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  \AddressEdges P&\coloneqq
    \left\{(p,q)\in P\times P\;\middle|\;
      \substack{\exists i\in\{0,1\}:\\[-2pt]
        q=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}\lor
        p=\WordConcat q{\LetterWord i}}\right\},\\[-2pt]
  \AddressInner P&\coloneqq
    \bigl\{p\in P\bigm|\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in P\bigr\},\\[-2pt]
  \AddressLeft P&\coloneqq
    \bigl\{(p,\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0})\bigm|
      p\in\AddressInner P\bigr\},\\[-2pt]
  \AddressRight P&\coloneqq
    \bigl\{(p,\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1})\bigm|
      p\in\AddressInner P\bigr\}.
  \end{aligned}%
}{BinaryAddressGraphDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{P\dsep p\dsep q\dsep i\dsep
    \AddressEdges P\dsep\AddressInner P}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\AddressLeft P\dsep\AddressRight P}
  \DeltaPrem{Volle Präfixmenge}{\GoodAddressSet P}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neue Symbole}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Adresskanten}{\AddressEdges P}
  \DeltaPrem{Innere Adressmenge}{\AddressInner P}
  \DeltaPrem{Linkes Adresskind}{\AddressLeft P}
  \DeltaPrem{Rechtes Adresskind}{\AddressRight P}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Volle Präfixmengen tragen endliche geordnete volle
Binärbäume]{%
  \GoodAddressSet P\vdash
  \Finite P\land
  \OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,
    \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft P,\AddressRight P}%
}{GoodBinaryAddressSetIsFiniteOrderedFullBinaryTree}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{P\dsep p\dsep q\dsep i\dsep z}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\AddressLeft P\dsep\AddressRight P}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Schlichter Adressgraph]{%
    \GoodAddressSet P\vdash
      \SimpleUndirectedGraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}
  }{\GoodAddressSet P\vdash
      \SimpleUndirectedGraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}}%
    [GoodBinaryAddressGraphSimple]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\AddressEdges P\subseteq P\times P}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{p,q\in P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{(p,q)\in\AddressEdges P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3,4]{(q,p)\in\AddressEdges P}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def]{1,3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall p,q\in P\,
      ((p,q)\in\AddressEdges P\rightarrow(q,p)\in\AddressEdges P)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{3,\rUI{\rRI{4,5}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{p\in P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{(p,p)\in\AddressEdges P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{%
      \exists i\in\{0,1\}\,p=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def]{1,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
      i\in\{0,1\}\land p=\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,7,10]{%
      \WordLength p=\WordLength p+1}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Aus \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def] ist
        \(p\in\FiniteWords{\{0,1\}}\). Für \(i\in\{0,1\}\) liefern\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm] und
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordFinite}[thm] die Typisierung des
        Buchstabenwortes und seine Länge eins;\\
        nun folgen die Gleichung aus Zeile 10 und
        \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm].}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
      \WordLength p+1\neq\WordLength p}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddPositiveDistance}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1,7}},
        \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax]},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOne}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,10]{%
      \WordLength p+1=\WordLength p}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,10]{\bot}{\rBI{13,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{\bot}{\rEE{9,10,14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{(p,p)\notin\AddressEdges P}{\rCI{8,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall p\in P\,((p,p)\notin\AddressEdges P)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{7,16}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \SimpleUndirectedGraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{SimpleUndirectedGraphIntroduction}[thm]{2,6,17}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutige Wurzelwege]{%
    \GoodAddressSet P\vdash
    \forall q\in P\,\exists!z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)
  }{%
    \GoodAddressSet P\vdash
    \forall q\in P\,\exists!z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)
  }[GoodBinaryAddressRootPathsUnique]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \SimpleUndirectedGraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressGraphSimple}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}}{%
      \rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{SimpleUndirectedGraphAccess}[thm]{2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{q\in P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,4]{q\in\BinaryAddresses}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1,4}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,4]{%
      \exists z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Die Folge der Anfangswörter von \(q\) liefert den
        Zeugen. Dies folgt durch Induktion über \(\WordLength q\);\\
        benutzt werden der Präfixabschluss aus
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def], die Endzerlegung aus
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecomposition}[thm]\\
        sowie \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def] und
        \FormulaRefAuto{DirectedPathDef}[def].}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{%
      z_1\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{%
      z_2\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,4,7,8]{z_1=z_2}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Jede Adresskante ändert die Wortlänge um eins.\\
        Beim ersten Abwärtsschritt eines einfachen Wurzelwegs\\
        wären sein Vorgänger und Nachfolger wegen\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecomposition}[thm] gleich.
        Daher steigen die Längen strikt.\\
        Jeder Weg ist die in Zeile 6 konstruierte eindeutige Folge\\
        der Anfangswörter.}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,4]{%
      \exists!z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)}{\UEI{6,7,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall q\in P\,\exists!z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{4,10}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Adresskinder]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\GoodAddressSet P\dsep p\in P\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad\TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
      =\{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}\mid i\in\{0,1\}\}\cap P
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\GoodAddressSet P\dsep p\in P\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad\TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
      =\{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}\mid i\in\{0,1\}\}\cap P
    \end{aligned}
  }[GoodBinaryAddressChildren]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{p\in P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \SimpleUndirectedGraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressGraphSimple}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in P}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall q\in P\,\exists!z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressRootPathsUnique}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \RootedTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{RootedTreeFromUniqueRootPaths}[thm]{3,4,5}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,2]{%
      \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
      =\{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}\mid i\in\{0,1\}\}\cap P}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Beide Inklusionen folgen aus\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeChildrenMembership}[thm] und
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeParentDef}[def].\\
        Im eindeutigen Wurzelweg aus\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressRootPathsUnique}[thm]\\
        ist der Elternknoten genau das um den letzten Buchstaben gekürzte
        Wort.\\
        Die Eindeutigkeit der Endzerlegung liefert\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecomposition}[thm].}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Kinder und Fülle]{%
    \GoodAddressSet P\vdash
      \FullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}
  }{\GoodAddressSet P\vdash
      \FullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}%
    [GoodBinaryAddressGraphFullBinary]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \SimpleUndirectedGraphStruct{P,\AddressEdges P}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressGraphSimple}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}\in P}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall q\in P\,\exists!z\,
      \bigl(z\in\GraphPathsBetween{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{q}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressRootPathsUnique}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \RootedTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{RootedTreeFromUniqueRootPaths}[thm]{2,3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{p\in P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
      \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
      =\{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord i}\mid i\in\{0,1\}\}\cap P}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressChildren}[thm]{1,6}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,6]{%
      \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
      \subseteq\{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0},
                   \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Aus \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressChildren}[thm]
        und dem Elementkriterium der durch \(i\in\{0,1\}\)
        beschriebenen Bildmenge.}}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,6]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall a,b,c\in
        \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}\,
        (&a=b\lor a=c\lor b=c)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{ThreeElementsInPair}[thm]{8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall p\in P\,\forall a,b,c\in
        \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}\,
        (a=b\lor a=c\lor b=c)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,9}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \BinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryTreeDef}[def]{5,10}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,6]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
        =\varnothing\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
        =\{\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0},
            \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\}
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Fallunterscheidung über
        \(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in P\). Die Äquivalenz aus
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]\\
        und \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressChildren}[thm] liefern im
        negativen Fall die leere, im positiven die angezeigte Zweiermenge.}}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,6]{%
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\neq\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressChildrenDistinct}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1},6}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,6]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
        =\varnothing\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists a,b\in
        \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}\,
        \Bigl(a\neq b\land
          \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
          =\{a,b\}\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Fallunterscheidung in Zeile 12. Im zweiten Fall sind
        \(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\) und
        \(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\)\\
        nach den Paarmengen-Zugriffssätzen Kinder; Zeile 13 liefert ihre
        Verschiedenheit.}}}
    \proofstepwidestarbreak[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall p\in P\,\Bigl(&
        \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
          =\varnothing\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists a,b\in
        \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}\,
        \bigl(a\neq b\land
          \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
          =\{a,b\}\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rUI{\rRI{6,14}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \FullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FullBinaryTreeDef}[def]{11,15}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Ordnung und Endlichkeit}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet P}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Finite P}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \FullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressGraphFullBinary}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      p\in\TreeInnerVertices{P}{\AddressEdges P}
        {\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,4]{%
      \WordConcat p{\LetterWord0},\WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in P}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Aus \FormulaRefAuto{TreeInnerVertexSetDef}[def] und
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafInnerDef}[def] ist die Kindermenge
        nichtleer. Mit
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressChildren}[thm]\\
        liegt daher mindestens eines der beiden Adresskinder in \(P\);
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def] liefert dann beide.}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall p\in
        \TreeInnerVertices{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}\,
        \bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in P\land
          \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in P\bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{4,5}}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1]{%
      \AddressInner P=
        \TreeInnerVertices{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Extensionalität mit
        \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def],
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeInnerVertexSetDef}[def],
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafInnerDef}[def] und\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressChildren}[thm]. Die
        Füllenäquivalenz aus
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetDef}[def]\\
        identifiziert die beiden möglichen Kinder.}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall p\in\AddressInner P\,
        \bigl(\WordConcat p{\LetterWord0}\in P\land
          \WordConcat p{\LetterWord1}\in P\bigr)}{\rIE{7,6}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1]{%
      \AddressLeft P,\AddressRight P\colon\AddressInner P\to P}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Je eine Anwendung von
        \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]
        auf Zeile 8 und\\
        die beiden Funktionsgraphen aus
        \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def].}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \AddressLeft P,\AddressRight P\colon
      \TreeInnerVertices{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}\to P}{%
      \rIE{7,9}}
    \proofstepwidestarforcebreak[1,4]{%
      \AddressLeft P(p)\neq\AddressRight P(p)\land
      \TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
      =\{\AddressLeft P(p),\AddressRight P(p)\}}{%
      \text{\makecell[l]{Aus \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressGraphDef}[def], Zeilen
        5 und 7 sowie
        \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressChildren}[thm];\\
        die Verschiedenheit liefert
        \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryAddressChildrenDistinct}[thm].}}}
    \proofstepwidestarbreak[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall p\in
        \TreeInnerVertices{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}\,
      \Bigl(&\AddressLeft P(p)\neq\AddressRight P(p)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\TreeChildren{P}{\AddressEdges P}{\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}{p}
          =\{\AddressLeft P(p),\AddressRight P(p)\}\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rUI{\rRI{4,11}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,
        \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft P,\AddressRight P}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{OrderedFullBinaryTreeDef}[def]{3,10,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \Finite P\land
      \OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{P,\AddressEdges P,
        \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft P,\AddressRight P}}{%
      \rAI{2,13}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\enlargethispage{3\baselineskip}
\FormulaDefDeltaK[Positionsgraph eines Klammerungsbaumcodes]{%
  R\in\BracketTreeSet A\vdash
  \TreePositionGraph A R\coloneqq
  \bigl(\TreePositions A R,\AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},
    \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft{\TreePositions A R},
    \AddressRight{\TreePositions A R}\bigr)%
}{TreePositionGraphDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep R\dsep\TreePositions A R}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Positionsgraph}{\TreePositionGraph A R}
}

\vspace{-\baselineskip}
\FormulaThmDeltaK[Klammerungsbaumcodes tragen endliche geordnete volle
Binärbäume]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
  R\in\BracketTreeSet A\vdash{}&
    \Finite{\TreePositions A R}\land{}\\[-2pt]
  &\OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{
    \TreePositions A R,\AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},
    \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft{\TreePositions A R},
    \AddressRight{\TreePositions A R}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
  &\TreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
    \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{BracketCodePositionGraphIsFiniteOrderedFullBinaryTree}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep R\dsep\TreePositions A R}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{R\in\BracketTreeSet A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\GoodAddressSet{\TreePositions A R}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreePositionSetIsGood}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestarbreak[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\Finite{\TreePositions A R}\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{
      \TreePositions A R,\AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},
      \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft{\TreePositions A R},
      \AddressRight{\TreePositions A R}}
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{GoodBinaryAddressSetIsFiniteOrderedFullBinaryTree}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Finite{\TreePositions A R}}{\rAEa{3}}
  \proofstepwidestarbreak[1]{%
    \OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{
      \TreePositions A R,\AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},
      \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft{\TreePositions A R},
      \AddressRight{\TreePositions A R}}}{\rAEb{3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\FullBinaryTreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
      \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\BinaryTreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
      \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\RootedTreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
      \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{OrderedFullBinaryTreeForgetfulChain}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \RootedTreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
      \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},\EmptyWord{\{0,1\}}}}{\rAEn{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
      \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R}}}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{RootedTreeAccess}[thm]{7}}}
  \proofstepwidestarbreak[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\Finite{\TreePositions A R}\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\OrderedFullBinaryTreeStruct{
      \TreePositions A R,\AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R},
      \EmptyWord{\{0,1\}},\AddressLeft{\TreePositions A R},
      \AddressRight{\TreePositions A R}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\TreeStruct{\TreePositions A R,
      \AddressEdges{\TreePositions A R}}
    \end{aligned}}{\rAIStar{4,5,8}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\chapter{Blattwörter, Klammerungen und Auswertung}

\section{Blattwort}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Einbuchstabenabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{ein}_A\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A},\\[-2pt]
    &\forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\coloneqq\LetterWord{a}\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{LetterWordFunctionDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{ein}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Einbuchstabenabbildung}{\operatorname{ein}_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \exists!F\,\Bigl(
      F\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A}\land
      \forall a\in A\,F(a)=\LetterWord{a}
    \Bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die Konkatenation ist ein binärer Operator auf
nichtleeren Wörtern]{%
  \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)%
}{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\frown}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationDef}[def]{2}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Blattwortabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\exists!F\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{F\dsep\operatorname{ein}_A\dsep\frown}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \operatorname{ein}_A\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFunctionDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\colon
      \NonemptyWords{A}\times\NonemptyWords{A}
      \to\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{1,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Blattwort eines Klammerungsbaums]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{wort}_A\coloneqq
      \iota F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Blattwort}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Rekursionsgleichungen des Blattwortes]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
}
\newcommand{\BXXVIIProofTreeLeafWordIota}{%
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordExistenceUnique}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{wort}_A\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\iota F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{wort}_A\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(\forall a\in A\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{1,2}}%
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung des Blattwortes]{%
    \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}
  }{\operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}%
    [TreeLeafWordTyping]
    \BXXVIIProofTreeLeafWordIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
        \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rAEa{3}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Blattgleichung]{%
    a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}
  }{a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}%
    [TreeLeafWordLeafEquation]
    \BXXVIIProofTreeLeafWordIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)}{%
      \rRE{5,\rUE{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{\operatorname{ein}_A(a)=\LetterWord{a}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFunctionDef}[def]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{6,7}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Knotengleichung]{%
    S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
  }{S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}%
    [TreeLeafWordNodeEquation]
    \BXXVIIProofTreeLeafWordIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
        \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rAEa{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
        =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
          (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})}{%
      \rAEb{\rAEb{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{4,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{4,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6,7]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})}{%
      \rRE{7,\rRE{6,\rUE{\rUE{5}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[6,7]{%
      \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{11,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6,7]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{10,12}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\section{Klammerungen eines Wortes}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Klammerungsmenge eines Nichtleerwortes]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordBracketings{A}{w}\coloneqq
  \bigl\{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\bigm|
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr\}%
}{WordBracketingSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Klammerungsmenge}{\WordBracketings{A}{w}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementkriterium der Klammerungsmenge]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \Bigl(T\in\WordBracketings{A}{w}\leftrightarrow
  \bigl(T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr)\Bigr)%
}{WordBracketingMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{w}
      =\{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\mid\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}=w\}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingSetDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    T\in\{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\mid\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}=w\}
      \leftrightarrow
    \bigl(T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
      x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    T\in\WordBracketings{A}{w}\leftrightarrow
    \bigl(T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr)}{%
    \rIE{2,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz einer Klammerung]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordBracketings{A}{w}\neq\varnothing%
}{WordBracketingExistence}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep a\dsep u\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordLeafEquation}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \rAI{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in
      \WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{2,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash A\neq\varnothing}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}\neq\varnothing\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,7}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[9]{u\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
    \exists T\,\bigl(T\in\WordBracketings{A}{u}\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\neq\varnothing\eqvdash
      \exists x\,(x\in A)}[thm]{11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[13]{T\in\WordBracketings{A}{u}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{%
    T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=u}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{9,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rAEa{14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=u}{\rAEb{14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafClosure}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeNodeClosure}[thm]{15,17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordLeafEquation}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
      {\TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordNodeEquation}[thm]{15,17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{u}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}}{%
    \rIE{16,20}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \rIE{19,21}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{9,23}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
      =\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \rAI{18,22}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}
    \in\WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{24,25}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash A\neq\varnothing}[thm]{26}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,11]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \rEE{12,13,27}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \rRI{11,28}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
    \forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
      \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
        \neq\varnothing\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{10,29}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
      \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
        \neq\varnothing\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{9,30}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[32]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[32]{\WordBracketings{A}{w}\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordInduction}[thm]{8,31,32}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Auswertung eines Klammerungsbaums}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Baumauswertung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep E\dsep\Id_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Id_A\colon A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IdA}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{2,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Auswertung eines Klammerungsbaums]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\coloneqq
      \iota E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Baumauswertung}{\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Rekursionsgleichungen der Baumauswertung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep
      S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
}
\newcommand{\BXXVIIProofTreeEvaluationIota}{%
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeEvaluationExistenceUnique}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{ev}_{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\iota E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeEvaluationDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(\forall a\in A\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{2,3}}%
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung der Baumauswertung]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A
  }{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}%
    [TreeEvaluationTyping]
    \BXXVIIProofTreeEvaluationIota
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}{\rAEa{4}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Blattgleichung der Baumauswertung]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a
  }{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a}%
    [TreeEvaluationLeafEquation]
    \BXXVIIProofTreeEvaluationIota
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{5}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\Id_A(a)=a}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash\Id_A(x)=x}[thm]{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{7,8}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Knotengleichung der Baumauswertung]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}
  }{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}%
    [TreeEvaluationNodeEquation]
    \BXXVIIProofTreeEvaluationIota
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}{\rAEa{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
        =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
          (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})}{%
      \rAEb{\rAEb{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{5,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{5,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})}{%
      \rRE{8,\rRE{7,\rUE{\rUE{6}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{%
      \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1,9,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{11,12}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

% nachfolgenden Bände hineinwirken.
\let\proofstepwidestar\BandTwentySevenOriginalProofStepWideStar
